HUTCN(
  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 40, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (post_unet_esa): ESA(
    (conv1): Conv2d(40, 10, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv2): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
    (conv3): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (conv4): Conv2d(10, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): P_HTCB(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(40, 10, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(10, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(

        (conv2): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(10, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(40, 10, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(10, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
    (tcn1): TCN(
      (dat): DAT(
        (blocks): ModuleList(
          (0-2): 3 x SWDABlock(
            (norm1): LayerNorm2d(
              (norm): LayerNorm((40,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            )
            (norm2): LayerNorm2d(
              (norm): LayerNorm((40,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            )
            (norm3): LayerNorm2d(
              (norm): LayerNorm((40,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            )
            (w_attn): WindowAttention(
              (qkv): Linear(in_features=40, out_features=120, bias=True)
              (proj): Linear(in_features=40, out_features=40, bias=True)
            )
            (sw_attn): WindowAttention(
              (qkv): Linear(in_features=40, out_features=120, bias=True)
              (proj): Linear(in_features=40, out_features=40, bias=True)
            )
            (ffn): ConvFFN(
              (pw1): Conv2d(40, 80, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
              (dw): Conv2d(80, 80, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=80)
              (pw2): Conv2d(80, 40, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
              (act): GELU(approximate='none')
            )
          )
        )
        (norm): LayerNorm2d(
          (norm): LayerNorm((40,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
        )
      )
      (cnn): Sequential(
        (0): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (1): GELU(approximate='none')
        (2): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (3): GELU(approximate='none')
        (4): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (5): GELU(approximate='none')
        (6): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (7): GELU(approximate='none')
        (8): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      )
    )
    (c): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (tesa_out): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(40, 10, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)

  (LR_conv2): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

  (ps_conv2): Conv2d(3, 3, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))

          (conv4): Conv2d(10, 40, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
          (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
          (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
        )
        (esa3): ESA(
          (conv1): Conv2d(40, 10, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
          (conv2): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
          (conv3): Conv2d(10, 10, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))

          (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
          (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
        )
      )
    )
    (LR_conv1): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (LR_conv2): Conv2d(40, 40, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
